var subjectFolder = location.pathname;
subjectFolder = subjectFolder.replace("/", "");
if (subjectFolder.startsWith("python/") == true ) {
  if (subjectFolder.includes("/numpy/") == true ) {
    subjectFolder = "numpy/"
  } else if (subjectFolder.includes("/pandas/") == true ) {
      subjectFolder = "pandas/"
  } else if (subjectFolder.includes("/scipy/") == true ) {
      subjectFolder = "scipy/"
  }
}
subjectFolder = subjectFolder.substr(0, subjectFolder.indexOf("/"));

<script>
(
function setThemeMode() {
  var x = localStorage.getItem("preferredmode");
  var y = localStorage.getItem("preferredpagemode");  
  if (x == "dark") {
    document.body.className += " darktheme";
  }
  if (y == "dark") {
    document.body.className += " darkpagetheme";
  }
})();
</script>

<script>
activate_subtopnav_scroll = 0;
from_scrollpos = -1;
current_scrollpos = -1;
goto_tut = 1;
function startscrolling_subtopnav(event) {
  event.preventDefault();
  from_scrollpos = event.clientX;
  activate_subtopnav_scroll = 1;
}
function scrolling_subtopnav(event) {
  current_scrollpos = event.clientX;
  if (current_scrollpos == from_scrollpos) return false;
  event.preventDefault();
  if (event.buttons == 0) return false;
  var scrollspeed;
  if (activate_subtopnav_scroll == 1) {
    goto_tut = 0;
    scrollspeed = current_scrollpos - from_scrollpos;
    scrollspeed = Math.abs(scrollspeed);
    if (current_scrollpos < from_scrollpos) {
     document.getElementById("scroll_left_btn").style.display = "block";     
     document.getElementById("subtopnav").scrollLeft += scrollspeed;        
    } else {
      document.getElementById("subtopnav").scrollLeft -= scrollspeed;        
    }
    scrollbtn_visible();
    from_scrollpos = current_scrollpos;
  }
}
function endscrolling_subtopnav(event) {
  event.preventDefault();
  activate_subtopnav_scroll = 0;
  from_scrollpos = -1;
  current_scrollpos = -1;
}
function pellessii(event) {
  if (goto_tut == 0) {
    event.preventDefault();  
    goto_tut = 1;
    return false;
  }
}
var scrollspeed = 1;
var scrollinterval
function scrollmenow(n) {
  scrollinterval = window.setInterval(function() {
    scrollspeed = scrollspeed * 1.1;
    if (scrollspeed > 10) {scrollspeed = 10;}
    if (n == 1) {
      document.getElementById("subtopnav").scrollLeft += scrollspeed;  
    } else {
      document.getElementById("subtopnav").scrollLeft -= scrollspeed;  
      
    }
    scrollbtn_visible();
  }, 10);
}

var p_s_l = 0;
var x_s_l = 0;
function scrollbtn_visible() {
  var c_s_l = document.getElementById("subtopnav").scrollLeft;
  if (c_s_l < 1) {
    document.getElementById("scroll_left_btn").style.display = "none";    
  } else {
    document.getElementById("scroll_left_btn").style.display = "block";    
  }
  if (c_s_l > 1 && c_s_l == p_s_l) {
    x_s_l++;
  } else {
    x_s_l = 0;  
  }
  if (x_s_l > 3) {
    document.getElementById("scroll_right_btn").style.display = "none";    
  } else {
    document.getElementById("scroll_right_btn").style.display = "block";  
  }
  p_s_l = document.getElementById("subtopnav").scrollLeft;
}

function stopscrollmenow() {
  scrollspeed = 1;
  window.clearInterval(scrollinterval);
}

window.addEventListener("load", scrollbtn_visible);

function subtopnav_intoview() {
  var a, b, c, d, i = 0;
  a = document.getElementById("subtopnav");
  if (!a || !a.getElementsByClassName) {return false;}
  var x = a.getElementsByTagName("A");
  b = document.location.pathname;
  if (b.indexOf("/python/numpy/") > -1 || b.indexOf("/python/pandas/") > -1 || b.indexOf("/python/scipy/") > -1) {
    for (i = 0; i < x.length; i++) {
      c = x[i].pathname;
      if ((b.indexOf("/python/numpy/") > -1 && c == "/python/numpy/default.asp") || (b.indexOf("/python/pandas/") > -1 && c == "/python/pandas/default.asp") || (b.indexOf("/python/scipy/") > -1 && c == "/python/scipy/index.php")) {
        x[i].className += " active";
        break;
      }
    }
  } else {
    for (i = 0; i < x.length; i++) {
      c = x[i].pathname;
      if (c.indexOf("void(0)") == -1 && b.substr(0, b.indexOf("/",1)) ==  c.substr(0, c.indexOf("/",1))) {
        x[i].className += " active";
        break;
      }
    }
  }
  d = a.getElementsByClassName("active");
  if (d.length < 1) {return false;}  
  while (!amIIntoView(a, d[0])) {
    i++
    if (i > 1000) {break;}
    a.scrollLeft += 10;
    //console.log(i);
  }
  //scrollbtn_visible();
}

function amIIntoView(x, y) {
  var a = x.scrollLeft;
  var width = Math.min(window.innerWidth, 1750);
  var b = a + width - 20;
  var ytop = y.offsetLeft;
  var c = y.offsetWidth;
  var d = 0;
  if (document.getElementById("btn_container_subtopnav")) d = document.getElementById("btn_container_subtopnav").offsetWidth;
  var ybottom = ytop + c + d + 20;
  return ((ybottom <= b) && (ytop >= a));
}

</script>

      <a class="ghost ga-fp ga-quiz-default" href="/quiztest/default.asp">Quizzes</a>

<script>
(function () {
  'use strict';

  var root = document.getElementById('w3-home-refresh');
  if (!root) return;

  var fadeItems = root.querySelectorAll('#home-app .fade');
  if ('IntersectionObserver' in window) {
    root.classList.add('is-enhanced');
    var observer = new IntersectionObserver(function (entries) {
      entries.forEach(function (entry) {
        if (entry.isIntersecting) {
          entry.target.classList.add('visible');
          observer.unobserve(entry.target);
        }
      });
    }, { threshold: 0.16 });
    fadeItems.forEach(function (item) { observer.observe(item); });
  } else {
    fadeItems.forEach(function (item) { item.classList.add('visible'); });
  }

  var topicCarousels = root.querySelectorAll('[data-topic-carousel]');
  topicCarousels.forEach(function (carousel) {
    var track = carousel.querySelector('[data-carousel-track]');
    var previous = carousel.querySelector('[data-carousel-prev]');
    var next = carousel.querySelector('[data-carousel-next]');
    var updateQueued = false;
    if (!track || !previous || !next) return;

    function updateCarousel() {
      updateQueued = false;
      var maxScroll = Math.max(0, track.scrollWidth - track.clientWidth);
      var atStart = track.scrollLeft <= 6;
      var atEnd = track.scrollLeft >= maxScroll - 6;
      previous.disabled = atStart;
      next.disabled = atEnd;
      carousel.classList.toggle('is-at-start', atStart);
      carousel.classList.toggle('is-at-end', atEnd);
    }

    function queueCarouselUpdate() {
      if (updateQueued) return;
      updateQueued = true;
      window.requestAnimationFrame(updateCarousel);
    }

    function moveCarousel(direction) {
      var distance = Math.max(240, Math.round(track.clientWidth * 0.72));
      if (typeof track.scrollBy === 'function') {
        track.scrollBy({ left: direction * distance, behavior: 'smooth' });
      } else {
        track.scrollLeft += direction * distance;
      }
      window.setTimeout(updateCarousel, 360);
    }

    previous.addEventListener('click', function () { moveCarousel(-1); });
    next.addEventListener('click', function () { moveCarousel(1); });
    track.addEventListener('scroll', queueCarouselUpdate, { passive: true });
    window.addEventListener('resize', queueCarouselUpdate);
    updateCarousel();
    window.setTimeout(updateCarousel, 50);
  });

  function escapeCodeText(value) {
    return String(value)
      .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
      .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
      .replace(/>/g, '&gt;');
  }

  function codeColorSpan(className, color, value) {
    return '<span class="' + className + '" style="color:' + color + '">' + escapeCodeText(value) + '</span>';
  }

  function highlightHtmlTag(tag) {
    if (/^<!doctype/i.test(tag)) {
      return codeColorSpan('doctypecolor', 'slategray', tag);
    }

    var match = tag.match(/^(<\/?)([A-Za-z][\w:-]*)([\s\S]*?)(\/?>)$/);
    if (!match) return escapeCodeText(tag);

    var output = codeColorSpan('tagcolor', '#999', match[1]);
    output += codeColorSpan('tagnamecolor', '#905', match[2]);

    var rest = match[3] || '';
    var attrRegex = /(\s+)([^\s=\/>]+)(\s*=\s*)("[^"]*"|'[^']*'|[^\s>]+)/g;
    var lastIndex = 0;
    var attrMatch;

    while ((attrMatch = attrRegex.exec(rest))) {
      output += escapeCodeText(rest.slice(lastIndex, attrMatch.index));
      output += escapeCodeText(attrMatch[1]);
      output += codeColorSpan('attributecolor', 'green', attrMatch[2]);
      output += escapeCodeText(attrMatch[3]);
      output += codeColorSpan('attributevaluecolor', 'mediumblue', attrMatch[4]);
      lastIndex = attrMatch.index + attrMatch[0].length;
    }

    output += escapeCodeText(rest.slice(lastIndex));
    output += codeColorSpan('tagcolor', '#999', match[4]);
    return output;
  }

  function highlightHtmlCode(rawCode) {
    return rawCode.split('\n').map(function (line) {
      var output = '';
      var lastIndex = 0;
      var tagRegex = /(<!DOCTYPE[^>]*>|<\/?[A-Za-z][^>]*>)/gi;
      var tagMatch;

      while ((tagMatch = tagRegex.exec(line))) {
        output += escapeCodeText(line.slice(lastIndex, tagMatch.index));
        output += highlightHtmlTag(tagMatch[0]);
        lastIndex = tagMatch.index + tagMatch[0].length;
      }

      output += escapeCodeText(line.slice(lastIndex));
      return output;
    }).join('<br>');
  }

  function buildKeywordMap(words) {
    var map = {};
    words.forEach(function (word) { map[word.toLowerCase()] = true; });
    return map;
  }

  function highlightProgramCode(rawCode, options) {
    var keywords = buildKeywordMap(options.keywords || []);
    var keywordClass = options.keywordClass || 'jskeywordcolor';
    var stringClass = options.stringClass || 'jsstringcolor';
    var numberClass = options.numberClass || 'jsnumbercolor';
    var commentClass = options.commentClass || 'commentcolor';

    return rawCode.split('\n').map(function (line) {
      var output = '';
      var index = 0;

      while (index < line.length) {
        var current = line.charAt(index);
        var next = line.charAt(index + 1);

        if (current === '/' && next === '/') {
          output += codeColorSpan(commentClass, 'green', line.slice(index));
          break;
        }
        if (options.hashComments && current === '#') {
          output += codeColorSpan(commentClass, 'green', line.slice(index));
          break;
        }
        if (current === '"' || current === "'" || current === '`') {
          var quote = current;
          var end = index + 1;
          var escaped = false;
          while (end < line.length) {
            var ch = line.charAt(end);
            if (!escaped && ch === quote) {
              end += 1;
              break;
            }
            if (!escaped && ch === '\\') {
              escaped = true;
            } else {
              escaped = false;
            }
            end += 1;
          }
          output += codeColorSpan(stringClass, 'brown', line.slice(index, end));
          index = end;
          continue;
        }
        if (/[0-9]/.test(current)) {
          var numberEnd = index + 1;
          while (numberEnd < line.length && /[0-9._]/.test(line.charAt(numberEnd))) numberEnd += 1;
          output += codeColorSpan(numberClass, 'red', line.slice(index, numberEnd));
          index = numberEnd;
          continue;
        }
        if (/[A-Za-z_$]/.test(current)) {
          var wordEnd = index + 1;
          while (wordEnd < line.length && /[A-Za-z0-9_$]/.test(line.charAt(wordEnd))) wordEnd += 1;
          var word = line.slice(index, wordEnd);
          if (keywords[word.toLowerCase()]) {
            output += codeColorSpan(keywordClass, 'mediumblue', word);
          } else {
            output += escapeCodeText(word);
          }
          index = wordEnd;
          continue;
        }

        output += escapeCodeText(current);
        index += 1;
      }

      return output;
    }).join('<br>');
  }

  function highlightExtraTopicCode(config) {
    if (config.syntax === 'html') return highlightHtmlCode(config.code);
    if (config.syntax === 'sql') {
      return highlightProgramCode(config.code, {
        keywords: ['SELECT', 'FROM', 'WHERE', 'AND', 'OR', 'INSERT', 'INTO', 'UPDATE', 'DELETE', 'CREATE', 'TABLE', 'VALUES', 'ORDER', 'BY', 'GROUP', 'JOIN', 'ON', 'AS', 'LIMIT'],
        keywordClass: 'sqlkeywordcolor',
        stringClass: 'sqlstringcolor',
        numberClass: 'sqlnumbercolor'
      });
    }
    if (config.syntax === 'python') {
      return highlightProgramCode(config.code, {
        keywords: ['for', 'in', 'if', 'elif', 'else', 'while', 'def', 'return', 'class', 'import', 'from', 'True', 'False', 'None', 'print'],
        hashComments: true
      });
    }
    if (config.syntax === 'javascript' || config.syntax === 'typescript') {
      return highlightProgramCode(config.code, {
        keywords: ['function', 'var', 'let', 'const', 'if', 'else', 'return', 'class', 'new', 'import', 'from', 'export', 'interface', 'type', 'extends', 'async', 'await', 'console']
      });
    }
    if (config.syntax === 'swift') {
      return highlightProgramCode(config.code, {
        keywords: ['let', 'var', 'func', 'if', 'else', 'for', 'in', 'return', 'class', 'struct', 'import', 'print']
      });
    }
    if (config.syntax === 'r') {
      return highlightProgramCode(config.code, {
        keywords: ['if', 'else', 'for', 'in', 'function', 'TRUE', 'FALSE', 'NULL']
      });
    }
    if (config.syntax === 'rust') {
      return highlightProgramCode(config.code, {
        keywords: ['fn', 'let', 'mut', 'if', 'else', 'for', 'in', 'while', 'loop', 'match', 'impl', 'struct', 'enum', 'use', 'mod', 'pub', 'return']
      });
    }
    return escapeCodeText(config.code).replace(/\n/g, '<br>');
  }

  var extraTopicExamples = {
    w3css: {
      name: 'W3.CSS',
      syntax: 'html',
      code: `<div class="w3-container w3-teal">

</div>

<div class="w3-container w3-teal">
\u00A0\u00A0<p>My Footer</p>
</div>`,
      tryUrl: '/w3css/tryit.asp?filename=tryw3css_default_intro',
      learnUrl: '/w3css/default.asp',
      referenceUrl: '/w3css/w3css_references.asp',
      certificateUrl: 'https://campus.w3schools.com/collections/certifications/products/w3-css-certificate'
    },
    bootstrap5: {

\u00A0\u00A0<h1>My First Bootstrap Page</h1>

<div class="container mt-5">
\u00A0\u00A0<div class="row">
\u00A0\u00A0\u00A0\u00A0<div class="col-sm-4">
\u00A0\u00A0\u00A0\u00A0\u00A0\u00A0<h3>Column 1</h3>
\u00A0\u00A0\u00A0\u00A0\u00A0\u00A0<p>Lorem ipsum dolor sit amet...</p>
\u00A0\u00A0\u00A0\u00A0</div>

  function renderExtraTopic(topicKey) {
    var config = extraTopicExamples[topicKey];
    if (!config) return false;
    var title = root.querySelector('#home-extra-example-title');
    var code = root.querySelector('#home-extra-example-code');
    var tryit = root.querySelector('#home-extra-tryit');
    var learn = root.querySelector('#home-extra-learn');
    var reference = root.querySelector('#home-extra-reference');
    var certificate = root.querySelector('#home-extra-certificate');
    var panel = root.querySelector('#home-panel-extra');
    if (!title || !code || !tryit || !learn || !reference || !certificate || !panel) return false;

    title.textContent = config.name + ' example';
    code.innerHTML = highlightExtraTopicCode(config);
    code.setAttribute('aria-label', config.name + ' code example');
    tryit.href = config.tryUrl;
    tryit.title = 'Try ' + config.name + ' Online';
    learn.href = config.learnUrl;
    learn.textContent = 'Learn ' + config.name;
    learn.title = config.name + ' Tutorial';
    reference.hidden = !config.referenceUrl;
    if (config.referenceUrl) {
      reference.href = config.referenceUrl;
      reference.textContent = config.name + ' Reference';
      reference.title = config.name + ' Reference';
    } else {
      reference.removeAttribute('href');
    }

    certificate.hidden = config.certificateUrl === null;
    if (!certificate.hidden) {
      certificate.href = config.certificateUrl || 'https://campus.w3schools.com/collections/certifications';
      certificate.textContent = config.name + ' Certificate';
      certificate.title = config.name + ' Certificate';
    } else {
      certificate.removeAttribute('href');
    }
    panel.setAttribute('aria-labelledby', 'home-tab-' + topicKey);
    return true;
  }

  var tabButtons = root.querySelectorAll('.tabbtn');
  var panels = root.querySelectorAll('.demo .panel');

  function activateTab(button) {
    tabButtons.forEach(function (item) {
      var active = item === button;
      item.classList.toggle('on', active);
      item.setAttribute('aria-selected', active ? 'true' : 'false');
      item.setAttribute('tabindex', active ? '0' : '-1');
    });
    var topicKey = button.getAttribute('data-t');
    var usesExtraPanel = renderExtraTopic(topicKey);
    panels.forEach(function (panel) {
      var activePanel = usesExtraPanel ? panel.id === 'home-panel-extra' : panel.getAttribute('data-p') === topicKey;
      panel.classList.toggle('on', activePanel);
    });
    var tabTrack = button.closest('[data-carousel-track]');
    if (tabTrack) {
      var targetLeft = button.offsetLeft - (tabTrack.clientWidth - button.offsetWidth) / 2;
      if (typeof tabTrack.scrollTo === 'function') {
        tabTrack.scrollTo({ left: Math.max(0, targetLeft), behavior: 'smooth' });
      } else {
        tabTrack.scrollLeft = Math.max(0, targetLeft);
      }
    }
  }

  tabButtons.forEach(function (button, index) {
    button.setAttribute('tabindex', button.classList.contains('on') ? '0' : '-1');
    button.addEventListener('click', function () { activateTab(button); });
    button.addEventListener('keydown', function (event) {
      if (event.key !== 'ArrowLeft' && event.key !== 'ArrowRight') return;
      event.preventDefault();
      var direction = event.key === 'ArrowRight' ? 1 : -1;
      var nextIndex = (index + direction + tabButtons.length) % tabButtons.length;
      var nextButton = tabButtons[nextIndex];
      activateTab(nextButton);
      nextButton.focus();
    });
  });

  var spaceTabButtons = root.querySelectorAll('[data-space-file]');
  var spaceCodePanes = root.querySelectorAll('[data-space-code]');

  function activateSpaceFile(button) {
    var fileName = button.getAttribute('data-space-file');
    spaceTabButtons.forEach(function (item) {
      var active = item === button;
      item.classList.toggle('on', active);
      item.setAttribute('aria-selected', active ? 'true' : 'false');
      item.setAttribute('tabindex', active ? '0' : '-1');
    });
    spaceCodePanes.forEach(function (pane) {
      pane.hidden = pane.getAttribute('data-space-code') !== fileName;
    });
  }

  spaceTabButtons.forEach(function (button, index) {
    button.addEventListener('click', function () { activateSpaceFile(button); });
    button.addEventListener('keydown', function (event) {
      if (event.key !== 'ArrowLeft' && event.key !== 'ArrowRight') return;
      event.preventDefault();
      var direction = event.key === 'ArrowRight' ? 1 : -1;
      var nextIndex = (index + direction + spaceTabButtons.length) % spaceTabButtons.length;
      activateSpaceFile(spaceTabButtons[nextIndex]);
      spaceTabButtons[nextIndex].focus();
    });
  });
}());
</script>

<script>
function openLangTab(evtEd, codeName) {
  var indexEditor, proglanguage, progtablinks;
  proglanguage = document.getElementsByClassName("proglang");
  for (indexEditor = 0; indexEditor < proglanguage.length; indexEditor++) {
    proglanguage[indexEditor].style.display = "none";
  }
  progtablinks = document.getElementsByClassName("codeeditorbr-tablink");
  for (indexEditor = 0; indexEditor < proglanguage.length; indexEditor++) {
    progtablinks[indexEditor].className = progtablinks[indexEditor].className.replace(" ws-grey", "");
  }
  document.getElementById(codeName).style.display = "block";
  evtEd.currentTarget.className += " ws-grey";
}


function open_search(elmnt, sfb) {
  var a = document.getElementById("googleSearch");
  if (a.style.visibility == "hidden" && a.style.display == "") {
    a.style.visibility = "";
    a.style.display = "none";
  }
  if (sfb == false) {
    if (a.style.display == "") {
      a.style.visibility = "";
      a.style.display = "none";
      //elmnt.innerHTML = "<i class='fa'>&#xe802;</i>";    
    } else {
      a.style.display = "";
      a.style.visibility == "hidden"
    if (window.innerWidth > 700) {
      a.style.width = "40%";
    } else {
      a.style.width = "80%";
    }
    window.setTimeout(function () {
          if (document.getElementById("gsc-i-id1")) {
            document.getElementById("gsc-i-id1").focus();
          }
        }, 400);
    }
  } else {
    if (a.style.display == "") {
      a.style.visibility = "";
      a.style.display = "none";
    } else {
      a.style.display = "";
      a.style.visibility == "hidden"
    }
    a.style.visibility = "hidden";
    a.style.display = ""; 
  }
}

if (typeof w3CodeColor === "function") {
  w3CodeColor();
}

var searchFromBox = false;
function click_learntocode_search_btn() {
  var x = document.getElementsByClassName("search_item");
  if (x.length == 0) {
    cc = 1;
  }
  for (i = 0; i < x.length; i++) {
    if (x[i].className.indexOf("search_active") > -1) {
      n = x[i].href;
      if (n.indexOf("search_entire_w3schools") > -1) {
        cc = 1;
      }
      break;
    }
  }
  if (cc == 1) {
    searchFromBox = true;
    submit_search_form();
  } else {
    window.location = n;
  }
}
function find_search_results(inp) {
  var a, val, i, l, resultIndexes = [], resultTexts = [], html = "", classAtt = "", pos1, posNext, cc, c0, c1, c2, searchArray = SearchSuggestions;
  a = document.getElementById("listofsearchresults");
  a.innerHTML = "";
  a.style.display = "none";
  document.getElementById("search2").style.borderBottomLeftRadius = "25px";
  val = inp.value.toUpperCase();
  if (val == "") return false;
  for (i = 0; i < searchArray.length; i++) {
    if (searchArray[i][0].toUpperCase().substr(0, val.length) == val || searchArray[i][2].toUpperCase().substr(0, val.length) == val) {
      if (resultTexts.indexOf(searchArray[i][2]) == -1) {
        resultIndexes.push(i);
        resultTexts.push(searchArray[i][2]);
        if (resultIndexes.length > 5) break;
      }
    }
  }
  for (i = 0; i < searchArray.length; i++) {
    if (resultIndexes.indexOf(i) == -1 && (searchArray[i][0].toUpperCase().indexOf(val) > -1 || searchArray[i][2].toUpperCase().indexOf(val) > -1)) {
      if (resultTexts.indexOf(searchArray[i][2]) == -1) {
        resultIndexes.push(i);
        resultTexts.push(searchArray[i][2]);        
        if (resultIndexes.length > 5) break;
      }
    }
  }
  //if (resultIndexes.length == 0) return false;
  document.getElementById("search2").style.borderBottomLeftRadius = "0";
  a.style.display = "block";
  for (i = 0; i < resultIndexes.length; i++) {
    cc = searchArray[resultIndexes[i]][2];
    pos1 = cc.toUpperCase().indexOf(val);
    dd = "";
    while (pos1 > -1) {
      c0 = cc.substr(0, pos1);
      c1 = "<span class='span_search'>" + cc.substr(pos1, val.length) + "</span>";
      c2 = cc.substr(pos1 + val.length);
      dd += c0 + c1;
      posNext = c2.toUpperCase().indexOf(val);
      if (posNext > -1) {
        cc = c2;
        pos1 = posNext;
      } else {
        cc = dd + c2;
        pos1 = -1;
      }
    }
    classAtt = "";
    if (html == "") classAtt = " search_active";
    html += "<a class='search_item" + classAtt + "' href='" + searchArray[resultIndexes[i]][1] + "'>" + cc + "</a>";
  }
  if (resultIndexes.length == 0) {
    classAtt = "";
    if (html == "") classAtt = " search_active";
    html += "<a class='search_item" + classAtt + "' onclick='click_search_w3schools_link(event)' href='search_entire_w3schools'>Search W3Schools</a>";
  }
  a.innerHTML = html;
}
function click_search_w3schools_link(event) {
  event.preventDefault();
  submit_search_form();
}
function key_pressed_in_search(event) {
  var x, n, nn, i, cc = 0;
  var keycode = event.keyCode;
  //console.log(keycode);
  if (keycode == 38 || keycode == 40) { //up || down
    x = document.getElementsByClassName("search_item");
    for (i = 0; i < x.length; i++) {
      if (x[i].className.indexOf("search_active") > -1) {
        x[i].className = "search_item";
        n = i;
        break;
      }
    }
    if (keycode == 38) {
      nn = n - 1;
      if (nn < 0) nn = 0;
    }
    if (keycode == 40) {
      nn = n + 1;
      if (nn >= x.length) nn = nn - 1; 
    }
    x[nn].className = "search_item search_active";
  }
  if (keycode == 13) {  //enter
    event.preventDefault();
    x = document.getElementsByClassName("search_item");
    if (x.length == 0) {
      cc = 1;
    }
    for (i = 0; i < x.length; i++) {
      if (x[i].className.indexOf("search_active") > -1) {
        n = x[i].href;
        if (n.indexOf("search_entire_w3schools") > -1) {
          cc = 1;
        }
        break;
      }
    }
    if (cc == 1) {
      searchFromBox = true;
      submit_search_form();
    } else {
      window.location = n;
    }
  }
}

function submit_search_form() {
    searchFromBox = true;
    gSearch();
    var delayInMilliseconds = 100; //0.1 second
    setTimeout(execute_google_search, delayInMilliseconds);
}

function execute_google_search() {
  if (typeof google == 'object') {
    google.search.cse.element.getElement("standard0").execute(document.getElementById("search2").value);
  } else {
    setTimeout(execute_google_search, 100);
  }  
}

document.body.addEventListener("click", function(event) {
  var a, x = event.srcElement;
  if (x.id == "search2" ||x.id == "learntocode_searchbtn" || x.id == "learntocode_searchicon" || x.classList.contains("search_item")) {
  } else {
    a = document.getElementById("listofsearchresults");
    if (!a) return false;
    a.innerHTML = "";
    a.style.display = "none";
    document.getElementById("search2").style.borderBottomLeftRadius = "25px";
    if (searchFromBox == true) {
      document.getElementById("googleSearch").style.display = "none";
      document.getElementById("googleSearch").style.visibility = "block";  
    }
    searchFromBox = false;
  }
});

/* =========================================================
   CLOUD MOVEMENT
   ========================================================= */

@keyframes adventureCloudDriftRight {

  from {
    transform:
      translateX(-8px)
      scale(var(--cloud-scale,1));
  }

  to {
    transform:
      translateX(14px)
      scale(var(--cloud-scale,1));
  }

}

@keyframes adventureCloudDriftLeft {

  from {
    transform:
      translateX(10px)
      scale(var(--cloud-scale,1));
  }

  to {
    transform:
      translateX(-10px)
      scale(var(--cloud-scale,1));
  }

}
